
### Event Categories


The following table lists the data source offered by this integration.

| Data Source | Description                          |
| ----------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `Network protocol analysis` | Corelight conn, dns, http, ssl and the other protocol logs provide a structured overview of network activity. |
| `Network intrusion detection system` | The suricata_corelight log carries Suricata IDS alerts (signature, category, severity). |
| `DNS records` | The dns log records queries, query types and answers observed on the network. |
| `Web logs` | The http log provides requested hosts, URIs, methods, user agents and status codes. |
| `SSL/TLS certificates` | The ssl log exposes TLS version, cipher, server name and JA3/JA3S fingerprints. |
| `Authentication logs` | The ssh, kerberos, ntlm and ldap logs record authentication attempts, the account used and their outcome. |
| `Mail server` | The smtp and smtp_links logs record the envelope, the headers and the links of the messages relayed. |
| `Binary file metadata` | The files and pe logs carry file hashes, MIME types and portable executable headers. |
| `File monitoring` | The smb_files log records the files opened, read, written, renamed and deleted over SMB shares. |
| `Digital certificate logs` | The x509 and known_certs logs describe the certificates observed on the network. |
| `Asset management` | The known_hosts, known_services, known_devices, known_names, known_domains, known_users and software logs inventory the entities seen on the network. |
| `Netflow/Enclave netflow` | The conn and conn_long logs summarize every connection with its endpoints, volumes and duration. |





In details, the following table denotes the type of events produced by this integration.

| Name | Values |
| ---- | ------ |
| Kind | `alert`, `enrichment` |
| Category | `authentication`, `email`, `file`, `host`, `intrusion_detection`, `network`, `threat` |
| Type | `change`, `connection`, `deletion`, `indicator`, `info`, `protocol`, `start` |




### Transformed Events Samples after Ingestion { data-search-exclude }

This section demonstrates how the raw logs will be transformed by our parsers. It shows the extracted fields that will be available for use in the [built-in detection rules](/xdr/features/detect/rules_catalog.md) and hunting activities in the [events page](/xdr/features/investigate/events.md). Understanding these transformations is essential for analysts to create effective detection mechanisms with [custom detection rules](/xdr/features/detect/sigma.md) and to leverage the full potential of the collected data.

### Extracted Fields

The following table lists the fields that are extracted, normalized under the ECS format, analyzed and indexed by the parser. It should be noted that infered fields are not listed.

| Name | Type | Description                |
| ---- | ---- | ---------------------------|
|`@timestamp` | `date` | Date/time when the event originated. |
|`corelight.analyzer.failure_data` | `keyword` | Excerpt of the data the analyzer failed on. |
|`corelight.analyzer.kind` | `keyword` | Kind of analyzer the event is about (protocol, file or packet). |
|`corelight.analyzer.name` | `keyword` | Name of the Zeek analyzer that reported the event, e.g. HTTP, SSL, PE. |
|`corelight.conn.history` | `keyword` | Zeek connection history string (sequence of connection state flags). |
|`corelight.conn.local_orig` | `boolean` | Whether the originator of the connection is inside the monitored network. |
|`corelight.conn.local_resp` | `boolean` | Whether the responder of the connection is inside the monitored network. |
|`corelight.conn.missed_bytes` | `long` | Number of bytes the sensor never saw, because of packet loss or a gap in capture. |
|`corelight.conn.orig_bytes` | `long` | Payload bytes sent by the originator, as reported by the TCP sequence numbers or the UDP lengths; absent when the connection carried no payload. |
|`corelight.conn.orig_names` | `array` | Names Corelight resolved for the originator address. |
|`corelight.conn.orig_names_source` | `keyword` | Source the names of the originator were resolved from, e.g. DNS, DHCP. |
|`corelight.conn.resp_bytes` | `long` | Payload bytes sent by the responder, as reported by the TCP sequence numbers or the UDP lengths; absent when the connection carried no payload. |
|`corelight.conn.resp_names` | `array` | Names Corelight resolved for the responder address. |
|`corelight.conn.resp_names_source` | `keyword` | Source the names of the responder were resolved from, e.g. DNS, DHCP. |
|`corelight.conn.spcap_rule` | `long` | Identifier of the Smart PCAP rule that decided to keep the packets of this connection. |
|`corelight.conn.spcap_trigger` | `keyword` | Smart PCAP trigger that fired for this connection, e.g. all-unencrypted. |
|`corelight.conn.spcap_url` | `keyword` | URL the packets kept by Smart PCAP can be downloaded from. |
|`corelight.conn.state` | `keyword` | Zeek connection state (conn_state), e.g. SF, OTH, S1. |
|`corelight.conn.suri_ids` | `array` | Identifiers of the Suricata alerts raised on this connection. |
|`corelight.conn.tunnel_parents` | `array` | Identifiers of the outer connections this one is encapsulated in. |
|`corelight.cotp.is_orig` | `boolean` | Whether the COTP message was sent by the originator of the connection. |
|`corelight.cotp.pdu_code` | `keyword` | COTP PDU type code, e.g. 0xe0 for a connection request. |
|`corelight.cotp.pdu_name` | `keyword` | COTP PDU type, e.g. CR_Connect_Request, DT_Data. |
|`corelight.dce_rpc.endpoint` | `keyword` | DCE/RPC endpoint (interface) being called, e.g. samr, drsuapi, svcctl. |
|`corelight.dce_rpc.named_pipe` | `keyword` | SMB named pipe the DCE/RPC call was carried over, e.g. \pipe\lsass. |
|`corelight.dce_rpc.operation` | `keyword` | DCE/RPC operation invoked on the endpoint, e.g. SamrEnumerateDomainsInSamServer. |
|`corelight.dce_rpc.rtt` | `float` | Round-trip time observed for the DCE/RPC call, in seconds. |
|`corelight.dhcp.agent_remote_id` | `keyword` | Remote ID the DHCP relay agent added to the request (option 82). |
|`corelight.dhcp.assigned_addr` | `ip` | Address the DHCP server assigned to the client. |
|`corelight.dhcp.circuit_id` | `keyword` | Circuit ID the DHCP relay agent added to the request (option 82). |
|`corelight.dhcp.client_fqdn` | `keyword` | FQDN the client advertised in the DHCP request. |
|`corelight.dhcp.client_message` | `keyword` | Message the client sent along with its request. |
|`corelight.dhcp.client_software` | `keyword` | Software the DHCP client identified itself with. |
|`corelight.dhcp.domain` | `keyword` | Domain name option offered in the DHCP lease. |
|`corelight.dhcp.lease_time` | `float` | Lease duration granted by the server, in seconds. |
|`corelight.dhcp.msg_orig` | `array` | Addresses each DHCP message was seen coming from, in the order of msg_types; they differ from the client when a relay is involved. |
|`corelight.dhcp.msg_types` | `array` | Sequence of DHCP message types seen in the exchange, e.g. REQUEST, ACK. |
|`corelight.dhcp.requested_addr` | `ip` | Address the client asked for. |
|`corelight.dhcp.server_message` | `keyword` | Message the server sent along with its reply. |
|`corelight.dhcp.server_software` | `keyword` | Software the DHCP server identified itself with. |
|`corelight.dhcp.subscriber_id` | `keyword` | Subscriber ID the DHCP relay agent added to the request (option 82). |
|`corelight.dnp3.block_type` | `keyword` | Kind of DNP3 object block, e.g. Object_Header, Object_Prefix. |
|`corelight.dnp3.execute_count` | `long` | Number of times the outstation was asked to execute the control. |
|`corelight.dnp3.fc_reply` | `keyword` | DNP3 function code carried by the reply, e.g. RESPONSE. |
|`corelight.dnp3.fc_request` | `keyword` | DNP3 function code carried by the request, e.g. READ or WRITE. |
|`corelight.dnp3.function_code` | `keyword` | DNP3 function code carried by the object header. |
|`corelight.dnp3.iin` | `long` | DNP3 internal indications bitmask returned by the outstation. |
|`corelight.dnp3.index_number` | `long` | Index of the DNP3 point the operation applies to. |
|`corelight.dnp3.object_type` | `keyword` | DNP3 object group and variation the request or reply refers to. |
|`corelight.dnp3.off_time` | `long` | Duration the point is to stay inactive, in milliseconds. |
|`corelight.dnp3.on_time` | `long` | Duration the point is to stay active, in milliseconds. |
|`corelight.dnp3.operation_type` | `keyword` | Operation the outstation was asked to perform on the point, e.g. LATCH_ON, PULSE_ON. |
|`corelight.dnp3.status_code` | `keyword` | Status the outstation returned for the control request. |
|`corelight.dnp3.trip_control_code` | `keyword` | Trip/close control code of the DNP3 control relay output block. |
|`corelight.dns.qclass` | `long` | Numeric DNS query class. |
|`corelight.dns.qtype` | `long` | Numeric DNS query type. |
|`corelight.dns.rcode` | `long` | Numeric DNS response code. |
|`corelight.dns.rejected` | `boolean` | Whether the resolver rejected the query. |
|`corelight.dns.z` | `long` | Reserved bit of the DNS header, as sent on the wire. |
|`corelight.encrypted_dns.match` | `keyword` | How the encrypted DNS resolver was recognised. |
|`corelight.enip.command` | `keyword` | EtherNet/IP command, e.g. SendRRData, RegisterSession. |
|`corelight.enip.command_code` | `keyword` | Numeric EtherNet/IP command code. |
|`corelight.enip.is_orig` | `boolean` | Whether the EtherNet/IP message was sent by the originator of the connection. |
|`corelight.enip.length` | `long` | Length of the EtherNet/IP command payload, in bytes. |
|`corelight.enip.options` | `keyword` | Options field of the EtherNet/IP encapsulation header. |
|`corelight.enip.sender_context` | `keyword` | Opaque value the sender uses to pair a reply with its request. |
|`corelight.enip.session_handle` | `keyword` | Session handle the device assigned to the connection. |
|`corelight.enip.status` | `keyword` | Status the device returned for the command. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.c2s_clr_ex` | `keyword` | Excerpt of the client-to-server payload that looked like cleartext. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.c2s_clr_frac` | `float` | Fraction of the client-to-server payload that looks like cleartext. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.c2s_enc_dev` | `float` | Deviation from the byte distribution expected of encrypted client-to-server traffic. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.c2s_enc_frac` | `float` | Fraction of the client-to-server payload that looks encrypted. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.c2s_pdu1_enc` | `boolean` | Whether the first client-to-server PDU already looks encrypted. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.c2s_size` | `long` | Number of client-to-server payload bytes the verdict was computed on. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.s2c_clr_ex` | `keyword` | Excerpt of the server-to-client payload that looked like cleartext. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.s2c_clr_frac` | `float` | Fraction of the server-to-client payload that looks like cleartext. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.s2c_enc_dev` | `float` | Deviation from the byte distribution expected of encrypted server-to-client traffic. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.s2c_enc_frac` | `float` | Fraction of the server-to-client payload that looks encrypted. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.s2c_pdu1_enc` | `boolean` | Whether the first server-to-client PDU already looks encrypted. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.s2c_size` | `long` | Number of server-to-client payload bytes the verdict was computed on. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.service` | `array` | Application protocols Zeek recognised on the connection, if any. |
|`corelight.etc_viz.viz_stat` | `keyword` | Two letters, one per direction, saying whether the traffic looked clear (c/C) or encrypted (e/E). |
|`corelight.files.analyzers` | `array` | File analyzers that ran on the file, e.g. MD5, SHA256, PE. |
|`corelight.files.conn_uids` | `array` | Identifiers of the connections the file was seen on. |
|`corelight.files.depth` | `long` | Depth of the file in the container it was extracted from. |
|`corelight.files.extracted` | `keyword` | Name of the file the sensor wrote the extracted content to. |
|`corelight.files.is_orig` | `boolean` | Whether the file was sent by the originator of the connection. |
|`corelight.files.local_orig` | `boolean` | Whether the file was sent from inside the monitored network. |
|`corelight.files.missing_bytes` | `long` | Bytes of the file the sensor never saw. |
|`corelight.files.overflow_bytes` | `long` | Bytes of the file dropped because the reassembly buffer was full. |
|`corelight.files.source` | `keyword` | Protocol over which the file was seen (e.g. HTTP, SMTP, FTP). |
|`corelight.files.timedout` | `boolean` | Whether the file analysis was abandoned on timeout. |
|`corelight.files.total_bytes` | `long` | Total size of the file as announced by the protocol, in bytes. |
|`corelight.ftp.arg` | `keyword` | Argument of the FTP command, when there is one. |
|`corelight.ftp.data_channel.orig_h` | `ip` | Source address of the data channel. |
|`corelight.ftp.data_channel.passive` | `boolean` | Whether the data channel was negotiated in passive mode. |
|`corelight.ftp.data_channel.resp_h` | `ip` | Destination address of the data channel. |
|`corelight.ftp.data_channel.resp_p` | `long` | Destination port of the data channel. |
|`corelight.ftp.reply_code` | `long` | Numeric reply code returned by the server. |
|`corelight.ftp.reply_msg` | `keyword` | Textual reply returned by the server. |
|`corelight.fuid` | `keyword` | Identifier Zeek gave the file, which joins this event to files.log and to the other logs that carried the same file. |
|`corelight.generic_dns_tunnels.bytes` | `long` | Volume of data carried over the suspected DNS tunnel, in bytes. |
|`corelight.generic_dns_tunnels.capture_secs` | `float` | Length of the observation window the detection was computed on, in seconds. |
|`corelight.http.client_headers` | `array` | Request headers, as name:value pairs. |
|`corelight.http.info_code` | `long` | Status code of the informational (1xx) response that preceded the final one. |
|`corelight.http.info_msg` | `keyword` | Reason phrase of the informational (1xx) response. |
|`corelight.http.orig_filenames` | `array` | Names of the files sent in the request. |
|`corelight.http.orig_fuids` | `array` | Identifiers of the files sent in the request. |
|`corelight.http.orig_mime_types` | `array` | MIME types of the files sent in the request. |
|`corelight.http.origin` | `keyword` | Value of the Origin: request header. |
|`corelight.http.proxied` | `array` | Proxy headers seen on the request, e.g. X-Forwarded-For. |
|`corelight.http.resp_filenames` | `array` | Names of the files returned in the response. |
|`corelight.http.resp_fuids` | `array` | Identifiers of the files returned in the response. |
|`corelight.http.resp_mime_types` | `array` | MIME types of the files returned in the response. |
|`corelight.http.server` | `keyword` | Value of the Server: response header. |
|`corelight.http.server_headers` | `array` | Response headers, as name:value pairs. |
|`corelight.http.status_msg` | `keyword` | Reason phrase returned with the status code, e.g. OK, Not Found. |
|`corelight.http.tags` | `array` | Tags Zeek attached to the transaction. |
|`corelight.http.trans_depth` | `long` | Position of this request inside the connection, starting at 1. |
|`corelight.intel.matched` | `array` | Intelligence framework indicator types that matched, e.g. Intel::DOMAIN. |
|`corelight.intel.node` | `keyword` | Zeek node that reported the match. |
|`corelight.intel.where` | `keyword` | Location in the connection where the intel indicator was seen (seen.where). |
|`corelight.ipsec.certificates` | `array` | Certificates exchanged during the negotiation. |
|`corelight.ipsec.doi` | `long` | Domain of interpretation of the IKEv1 security association payload. |
|`corelight.ipsec.exchange_type` | `long` | IKE exchange type of the message. |
|`corelight.ipsec.flag_a` | `boolean` | IKE authentication-only flag. |
|`corelight.ipsec.flag_c` | `boolean` | IKE commit flag. |
|`corelight.ipsec.flag_e` | `boolean` | IKE encryption flag. |
|`corelight.ipsec.flag_i` | `boolean` | IKE initiator flag. |
|`corelight.ipsec.flag_r` | `boolean` | IKE response flag. |
|`corelight.ipsec.flag_v` | `boolean` | IKE version flag. |
|`corelight.ipsec.hash` | `keyword` | Hash payload carried by the exchange. |
|`corelight.ipsec.initiator_spi` | `keyword` | Security parameter index chosen by the initiator. |
|`corelight.ipsec.is_orig` | `boolean` | Whether the message was sent by the originator of the connection. |
|`corelight.ipsec.ke_dh_groups` | `array` | Diffie-Hellman groups offered in the key exchange payload. |
|`corelight.ipsec.length` | `long` | Length of the IKE message, in bytes. |
|`corelight.ipsec.maj_ver` | `long` | Major version of the IKE protocol. |
|`corelight.ipsec.message_id` | `long` | Identifier of the IKE message inside the exchange. |
|`corelight.ipsec.min_ver` | `long` | Minor version of the IKE protocol. |
|`corelight.ipsec.notify_messages` | `array` | Notify payloads carried by the exchange. |
|`corelight.ipsec.proposals` | `array` | Security association proposals offered. |
|`corelight.ipsec.protocol_id` | `long` | Protocol identifier of the proposal, e.g. ISAKMP, ESP or AH. |
|`corelight.ipsec.responder_spi` | `keyword` | Security parameter index chosen by the responder. |
|`corelight.ipsec.situation` | `keyword` | Situation field of the IKEv1 security association payload. |
|`corelight.ipsec.transform_attributes` | `array` | Attributes of the proposed transforms, e.g. encryption algorithm and key length. |
|`corelight.ipsec.transforms` | `array` | Transforms offered inside the proposals. |
|`corelight.ipsec.vendor_ids` | `array` | Vendor identification payloads, which often reveal the VPN implementation. |
|`corelight.kerberos.auth_ticket` | `keyword` | Hash of the ticket presented with the request. |
|`corelight.kerberos.cipher` | `keyword` | Ticket encryption type negotiated for the Kerberos exchange, e.g. aes256-cts-hmac-sha1-96, rc4-hmac. |
|`corelight.kerberos.forwardable` | `boolean` | Whether the requested Kerberos ticket is forwardable. |
|`corelight.kerberos.from` | `date` | Start of the validity window requested for the Kerberos ticket. |
|`corelight.kerberos.new_ticket` | `keyword` | Hash of the ticket the KDC issued. |
|`corelight.kerberos.renewable` | `boolean` | Whether the requested Kerberos ticket is renewable. |
|`corelight.kerberos.request_type` | `keyword` | Kerberos request type, AS for the initial authentication or TGS for a service ticket request. |
|`corelight.kerberos.service` | `keyword` | Service principal the ticket was requested for, e.g. LDAP/dc.windomain.local/windomain.local. |
|`corelight.kerberos.till` | `date` | Expiration time requested for the Kerberos ticket. |
|`corelight.known.annotations` | `array` | Annotations Corelight attached to the observed entity. |
|`corelight.known.application` | `array` | Application Corelight recognised behind the service. |
|`corelight.known.conns_closed` | `long` | Connections closed during the observation window. |
|`corelight.known.conns_opened` | `long` | Connections opened during the observation window. |
|`corelight.known.conns_pending` | `long` | Connections still pending at the end of the observation window. |
|`corelight.known.criticality` | `keyword` | Criticality the Corelight entity inventory assigns to the host. |
|`corelight.known.description` | `keyword` | Description the Corelight entity inventory holds for the host. |
|`corelight.known.domain` | `keyword` | Domain name the entity inventory has observed. |
|`corelight.known.host_type` | `keyword` | Role Corelight inferred for the observed host, e.g. server or client. |
|`corelight.known.last_active_interval` | `float` | Length of the observation window, in seconds. |
|`corelight.known.last_active_session` | `keyword` | Last session in which the entity was seen active. |
|`corelight.known.long_conns` | `long` | Connections that outlived the observation window. |
|`corelight.known.num_conns` | `long` | Number of connections seen for the entity during the observation window. |
|`corelight.known.num_conns_complete` | `long` | Number of connections to the service that completed. |
|`corelight.known.num_conns_pending` | `long` | Number of connections to the service still pending. |
|`corelight.known.num_instances` | `long` | Number of times the entity was seen during the observation window. |
|`corelight.known.protocols` | `array` | Protocols the device was observed speaking. |
|`corelight.known.service` | `array` | Application protocols observed on the port. |
|`corelight.known.software` | `array` | Software banner observed for the service. |
|`corelight.known.source` | `keyword` | Source the Corelight entity inventory got its information from. |
|`corelight.known.status` | `keyword` | Status the Corelight entity inventory holds for the host. |
|`corelight.known.vendor_mac` | `keyword` | Vendor the MAC address of the device belongs to. |
|`corelight.kuid` | `keyword` | Corelight identifier of the observed entity, used to correlate the known_* logs. |
|`corelight.ldap.argument` | `keyword` | Argument of the LDAP operation. Corelight redacts credentials, emitting REDACTED for a bind password. |
|`corelight.ldap.message_id` | `long` | LDAP message identifier, used to correlate a request with its response. |
|`corelight.ldap.object` | `keyword` | Object the LDAP operation applies to, e.g. the bind distinguished name. |
|`corelight.ldap.opcode` | `keyword` | LDAP operation performed, e.g. bind simple, bind SASL, modify, delete. |
|`corelight.ldap.result` | `keyword` | LDAP result code name, e.g. success, invalid credentials, no such object. |
|`corelight.ldap.version` | `long` | LDAP protocol version announced by the client, usually 3. |
|`corelight.ldap_search.attributes` | `array` | Attributes the search asked the directory to return. |
|`corelight.ldap_search.base_object` | `keyword` | Base distinguished name the LDAP search starts from, e.g. DC=windomain,DC=local. |
|`corelight.ldap_search.deref_aliases` | `keyword` | Alias dereferencing policy of the LDAP search, e.g. never, in searching, always. |
|`corelight.ldap_search.filter` | `keyword` | LDAP search filter. Broad or recursive filters are a common directory enumeration signal. |
|`corelight.ldap_search.result_count` | `long` | Number of entries returned by the LDAP search. |
|`corelight.ldap_search.scope` | `keyword` | Scope of the LDAP search, e.g. base, single, tree. |
|`corelight.modbus.address` | `long` | Address of the first register or coil the operation applies to. |
|`corelight.modbus.delta` | `long` | Difference between the old and the new value of the tracked register. |
|`corelight.modbus.device_id_code` | `long` | Read device identification code the request asked for. |
|`corelight.modbus.exception` | `keyword` | Modbus exception returned by the device, NONE when the request succeeded. |
|`corelight.modbus.function` | `keyword` | Modbus function invoked, e.g. READ_HOLDING_REGISTERS. |
|`corelight.modbus.is_orig` | `boolean` | Whether the Modbus message was sent by the originator of the connection. |
|`corelight.modbus.mei_type` | `keyword` | Modbus encapsulated interface type of the request. |
|`corelight.modbus.new_value` | `long` | Value held by the tracked register after the write. |
|`corelight.modbus.object_id` | `keyword` | Identifier of the device identification object returned. |
|`corelight.modbus.object_id_code` | `keyword` | Numeric code of the device identification object returned. |
|`corelight.modbus.old_value` | `long` | Value held by the tracked register before the write. |
|`corelight.modbus.pdu_type` | `keyword` | Kind of Modbus PDU, e.g. READ_HOLDING_REGISTERS. |
|`corelight.modbus.quantity` | `long` | Number of registers or coils the operation applies to. |
|`corelight.modbus.register` | `long` | Register the request addressed. |
|`corelight.modbus.request_response` | `keyword` | Whether the message is a request or a response. |
|`corelight.modbus.tid` | `long` | Modbus transaction identifier, which pairs a request with its reply. |
|`corelight.modbus.track_address` | `long` | Address Corelight is tracking the value of. |
|`corelight.modbus.unit` | `long` | Modbus unit identifier the request is addressed to. |
|`corelight.modbus.values` | `keyword` | Values read from or written to the registers. |
|`corelight.mysql.arg` | `keyword` | Argument of the MySQL command, typically the SQL statement. |
|`corelight.mysql.response` | `keyword` | Response the server returned for the command. |
|`corelight.mysql.rows` | `long` | Number of rows the query returned. |
|`corelight.notice.actions` | `array` | Actions the notice framework took, e.g. Notice::ACTION_LOG. |
|`corelight.notice.file_desc` | `keyword` | Description of the file the notice is about. |
|`corelight.notice.message` | `keyword` | Human-readable Zeek notice message (msg field), e.g. "SMB::FILE_WRITE to admin file share". |
|`corelight.notice.peer_descr` | `keyword` | Description of the Zeek worker that raised the notice. |
|`corelight.notice.severity` | `keyword` | Severity Corelight assigned to the notice. |
|`corelight.notice.sub` | `keyword` | Sub-message of a Zeek notice (sub field). |
|`corelight.notice.suppress_for` | `float` | Duration identical notices are suppressed for, in seconds. |
|`corelight.ntlm.server_nb_computer_name` | `keyword` | NetBIOS computer name advertised by the server in the NTLM challenge. |
|`corelight.ntlm.server_tree_name` | `keyword` | DNS forest (tree) name advertised by the server in the NTLM challenge. |
|`corelight.ntp.mode` | `long` | NTP association mode, e.g. 3 for client and 4 for server. |
|`corelight.ntp.num_exts` | `long` | Number of extension fields carried by the packet. |
|`corelight.ntp.org_time` | `date` | Originate timestamp: when the client sent its request. |
|`corelight.ntp.poll` | `float` | Polling interval the peer requested, in seconds. |
|`corelight.ntp.precision` | `float` | Precision of the server clock, in seconds. |
|`corelight.ntp.rec_time` | `date` | Receive timestamp: when the server received the request. |
|`corelight.ntp.ref_id` | `keyword` | Reference identifier of the upstream clock the server synchronises on. |
|`corelight.ntp.ref_time` | `date` | Reference timestamp: when the server clock was last set. |
|`corelight.ntp.root_delay` | `float` | Round-trip delay to the reference clock, in seconds. |
|`corelight.ntp.root_disp` | `float` | Dispersion relative to the reference clock, in seconds. |
|`corelight.ntp.stratum` | `long` | Distance of the server from the reference clock, in hops. |
|`corelight.ntp.version` | `long` | Version of the NTP protocol used. |
|`corelight.ntp.xmt_time` | `date` | Transmit timestamp: when the server sent its reply. |
|`corelight.ocsp.cert_status` | `keyword` | Revocation status returned for the certificate: good, revoked or unknown. |
|`corelight.ocsp.hash_algorithm` | `keyword` | Hash algorithm used to build the issuer hashes. |
|`corelight.ocsp.issuer_key_hash` | `keyword` | Hash of the issuer public key. |
|`corelight.ocsp.issuer_name_hash` | `keyword` | Hash of the issuer distinguished name. |
|`corelight.ocsp.next_update` | `date` | Time at which newer revocation information will be available. |
|`corelight.ocsp.revoke_reason` | `keyword` | Reason the certificate was revoked, when it was. |
|`corelight.ocsp.revoke_time` | `date` | Time at which the certificate was revoked. |
|`corelight.ocsp.serial_number` | `keyword` | Serial number of the certificate the response is about. |
|`corelight.ocsp.this_update` | `date` | Time at which the returned revocation status was known to be correct. |
|`corelight.pe.compile_ts` | `date` | Compilation timestamp recorded in the PE header. |
|`corelight.pe.has_cert_table` | `boolean` | Whether the executable carries a certificate table. |
|`corelight.pe.has_debug_data` | `boolean` | Whether the executable carries debug data. |
|`corelight.pe.has_export_table` | `boolean` | Whether the executable carries an export table. |
|`corelight.pe.has_import_table` | `boolean` | Whether the executable carries an import table. |
|`corelight.pe.is_64bit` | `boolean` | Whether the executable is 64-bit. |
|`corelight.pe.is_exe` | `boolean` | Whether the file is an executable rather than a library. |
|`corelight.pe.os` | `keyword` | Operating system the executable targets. |
|`corelight.pe.subsystem` | `keyword` | Subsystem the executable runs under. |
|`corelight.pe.uses_aslr` | `boolean` | Whether the executable opts into address space layout randomization. |
|`corelight.pe.uses_code_integrity` | `boolean` | Whether the executable requires code integrity checks. |
|`corelight.pe.uses_dep` | `boolean` | Whether the executable opts into data execution prevention. |
|`corelight.pe.uses_seh` | `boolean` | Whether the executable uses structured exception handling. |
|`corelight.quic.client_initial_dcid` | `keyword` | Destination connection identifier of the client Initial packet. |
|`corelight.quic.client_scid` | `keyword` | Source connection identifier chosen by the client. |
|`corelight.quic.history` | `keyword` | Sequence of QUIC packet types seen on the connection. |
|`corelight.quic.server_scid` | `keyword` | Source connection identifier chosen by the server. |
|`corelight.quic.version` | `keyword` | QUIC version used on the connection. |
|`corelight.rdp.cert_count` | `long` | Number of certificates the server sent. |
|`corelight.rdp.cert_permanent` | `boolean` | Whether the server certificate is a permanent one. |
|`corelight.rdp.cert_type` | `keyword` | Type of certificate the server sent. |
|`corelight.rdp.channels_joined` | `long` | Number of RDP virtual channels joined during the session. |
|`corelight.rdp.client_build` | `keyword` | Build number reported by the client. |
|`corelight.rdp.client_dig_product_id` | `keyword` | Product identifier the RDP client reported. |
|`corelight.rdp.client_name` | `keyword` | Machine name reported by the client. |
|`corelight.rdp.cookie` | `keyword` | Cookie sent in the connection request; it usually carries the account being used (mstshash). |
|`corelight.rdp.desktop_height` | `long` | Height of the requested desktop, in pixels. |
|`corelight.rdp.desktop_width` | `long` | Width of the requested desktop, in pixels. |
|`corelight.rdp.encryption_level` | `keyword` | Encryption level negotiated for the session. |
|`corelight.rdp.encryption_method` | `keyword` | Encryption method negotiated for the session. |
|`corelight.rdp.inferences` | `array` | Behavioural inferences Corelight drew from the session. |
|`corelight.rdp.keyboard_layout` | `keyword` | Keyboard layout advertised by the client, which hints at its locale. |
|`corelight.rdp.rdfp_hash` | `keyword` | Corelight RDP fingerprint (RDFP) of the client. |
|`corelight.rdp.rdfp_string` | `keyword` | Raw string the RDP fingerprint is computed from. |
|`corelight.rdp.requested_color_depth` | `keyword` | Colour depth the client asked for. |
|`corelight.rdp.result` | `keyword` | Outcome reported for the connection request, e.g. encrypted, success, failure. |
|`corelight.rdp.security_protocol` | `keyword` | Security protocol selected for the session, e.g. HYBRID, SSL, RDP. |
|`corelight.s7comm.error_class` | `keyword` | Class of the error returned by the PLC, 0 when there is none. |
|`corelight.s7comm.error_code` | `keyword` | Code of the error returned by the PLC, 0 when there is none. |
|`corelight.s7comm.function_code` | `keyword` | Numeric S7comm function code. |
|`corelight.s7comm.function_name` | `keyword` | S7comm function invoked, e.g. read-var or download-block. |
|`corelight.s7comm.is_orig` | `boolean` | Whether the message was sent by the originator of the connection. |
|`corelight.s7comm.method` | `keyword` | S7comm method the request used, e.g. Read SZL. |
|`corelight.s7comm.pdu_reference` | `long` | Reference linking an S7comm request to its reply. |
|`corelight.s7comm.return_code` | `keyword` | Return code the PLC answered with. |
|`corelight.s7comm.return_code_name` | `keyword` | Meaning of the return code the PLC answered with. |
|`corelight.s7comm.rosctr_code` | `long` | Numeric S7comm remote operating service control code. |
|`corelight.s7comm.rosctr_name` | `keyword` | S7comm message class, e.g. job, ack-data or userdata. |
|`corelight.s7comm.subfunction_code` | `keyword` | Numeric S7comm subfunction code. |
|`corelight.s7comm.subfunction_name` | `keyword` | S7comm subfunction invoked, when the function has one. |
|`corelight.s7comm.szl_id` | `keyword` | Identifier of the system status list (SZL) being read. |
|`corelight.s7comm.szl_id_name` | `keyword` | Name of the system status list (SZL) being read. |
|`corelight.s7comm.szl_index` | `keyword` | Index inside the system status list being read. |
|`corelight.smb_files.data_len_req` | `long` | Number of bytes requested by the client. |
|`corelight.smb_files.data_len_rsp` | `long` | Number of bytes returned in the SMB response, in bytes. |
|`corelight.smb_files.data_offset_req` | `long` | Offset requested by the client in the file. |
|`corelight.smb_files.previous_name` | `keyword` | Name the file had before it was renamed. |
|`corelight.smb_mapping.native_file_system` | `keyword` | File system backing the share. |
|`corelight.smb_mapping.object_path` | `keyword` | Share the client mapped. |
|`corelight.smb_mapping.path` | `keyword` | Share the client mapped, as reported by sensors that use the "path" field name. |
|`corelight.smb_mapping.service` | `keyword` | Service the share exposes, as announced by the server. |
|`corelight.smb_mapping.share_type` | `keyword` | Type of share: DISK, PIPE or PRINT. |
|`corelight.smtp.date` | `keyword` | Date header of the message, verbatim (RFC 5322). |
|`corelight.smtp.domains` | `array` | Domains found in the message body. |
|`corelight.smtp.first_received` | `keyword` | First Received header of the mail, in its RFC 5322 form. |
|`corelight.smtp.fuids` | `array` | Zeek file identifiers of the attachments carried by the message. |
|`corelight.smtp.helo` | `keyword` | Argument of the HELO or EHLO command sent by the client. |
|`corelight.smtp.in_reply_to` | `keyword` | Message the mail replies to, from the In-Reply-To header. |
|`corelight.smtp.is_webmail` | `boolean` | Whether the message was submitted through a webmail interface. |
|`corelight.smtp.last_reply` | `keyword` | Last server reply seen in the SMTP transaction. |
|`corelight.smtp.path` | `array` | Chain of hosts the message transited through, read from the Received: headers. |
|`corelight.smtp.rcptto` | `array` | Envelope recipients of the message, from the RCPT TO commands. |
|`corelight.smtp.second_received` | `keyword` | Second Received header of the mail, in its RFC 5322 form. |
|`corelight.smtp.trans_depth` | `long` | Rank of the transaction inside the SMTP connection. |
|`corelight.smtp.urls` | `array` | URLs found in the message body. |
|`corelight.smtp.x_originating_ip` | `ip` | Address carried by the X-Originating-IP header. |
|`corelight.snmp.display_string` | `keyword` | sysDescr string returned by the agent, which identifies the device. |
|`corelight.snmp.get_bulk_requests` | `long` | Number of GetBulk requests seen. |
|`corelight.snmp.get_requests` | `long` | Number of Get requests seen. |
|`corelight.snmp.get_responses` | `long` | Number of responses seen. |
|`corelight.snmp.set_requests` | `long` | Number of Set requests seen, which write to the device. |
|`corelight.snmp.up_since` | `date` | Time the agent reports having been up since. |
|`corelight.snmp.version` | `keyword` | SNMP version used: 1, 2c or 3. |
|`corelight.software.host` | `keyword` | Host the software was observed on, as it appeared in the traffic. |
|`corelight.software.port` | `long` | Port the software was observed on. |
|`corelight.software.type` | `keyword` | Category Zeek assigned to the software, e.g. HTTP::SERVER, HTTP::BROWSER. |
|`corelight.software.unparsed_version` | `keyword` | Version banner as seen in the traffic, before parsing. |
|`corelight.ssh.auth_attempts` | `long` | Number of authentication attempts Zeek observed on the SSH session. |
|`corelight.ssh.cipher_alg` | `keyword` | Symmetric cipher negotiated for the SSH session, e.g. chacha20-poly1305@openssh.com. |
|`corelight.ssh.client` | `keyword` | SSH client version string, e.g. SSH-2.0-OpenSSH_9.0. |
|`corelight.ssh.client_host_key_algorithms` | `keyword` | Host key algorithms the client accepts. |
|`corelight.ssh.compression_alg` | `keyword` | Compression algorithm negotiated for the SSH session. |
|`corelight.ssh.hassh` | `keyword` | HASSH fingerprint of the SSH client, derived from its algorithm offer. Identifies the client implementation even when the version string is spoofed. |
|`corelight.ssh.hassh_algorithms` | `keyword` | Algorithm list the client offered, from which its HASSH is computed. |
|`corelight.ssh.hassh_server` | `keyword` | HASSH fingerprint of the SSH server (hasshServer). |
|`corelight.ssh.hassh_server_algorithms` | `keyword` | Algorithm list the server offered, from which its HASSH is computed. |
|`corelight.ssh.hassh_version` | `keyword` | Version of the HASSH algorithm used to compute the fingerprints. |
|`corelight.ssh.host_key` | `keyword` | Fingerprint of the SSH server host key. A change on a known server is a man-in-the-middle signal. |
|`corelight.ssh.host_key_alg` | `keyword` | Algorithm of the SSH server host key, e.g. ssh-ed25519, ssh-rsa. |
|`corelight.ssh.inferences` | `array` | Behavioural inferences Corelight drew from the session. |
|`corelight.ssh.kex_alg` | `keyword` | Key exchange algorithm negotiated for the SSH session. |
|`corelight.ssh.mac_alg` | `keyword` | Message authentication algorithm negotiated for the SSH session. |
|`corelight.ssh.server` | `keyword` | SSH server version string, e.g. SSH-2.0-OpenSSH_8.9p1. |
|`corelight.ssh.server_host_key_algorithms` | `keyword` | Host key algorithms the server offers. |
|`corelight.ssh.version` | `long` | SSH protocol version, 1 or 2. |
|`corelight.ssl.cert_chain_fps` | `array` | SHA-1 fingerprints of the certificates the server presented, from leaf to root. |
|`corelight.ssl.client_cert_chain_fps` | `array` | SHA-1 fingerprints of the certificates the client presented. |
|`corelight.ssl.history` | `keyword` | Letters summarising the TLS handshake messages seen, in order. |
|`corelight.ssl.last_alert` | `keyword` | Last TLS alert exchanged on the connection. |
|`corelight.ssl.sni_matches_cert` | `boolean` | Whether the server name the client asked for matches the certificate served. |
|`corelight.ssl.validation_status` | `keyword` | TLS certificate chain validation status (e.g. ok, self signed certificate, unable to get local issuer certificate). |
|`corelight.suricata.flow_id` | `long` | Suricata flow identifier the alert belongs to. |
|`corelight.suricata.flow_metadata` | `keyword` | Metadata Suricata attached to the flow. |
|`corelight.suricata.gid` | `long` | Generator identifier of the rule that fired. |
|`corelight.suricata.icmp_code` | `long` | ICMP code of the packet the alert was raised on. |
|`corelight.suricata.icmp_type` | `long` | ICMP type of the packet the alert was raised on. |
|`corelight.suricata.metadata` | `array` | Metadata attached to the rule that fired. |
|`corelight.suricata.payload` | `keyword` | Payload the alert fired on, base64 encoded. |
|`corelight.suricata.payload_printable` | `keyword` | Printable form of the payload the alert fired on. |
|`corelight.suricata.suri_id` | `keyword` | Identifier of the alert, which joins it to the conn.log entry of the same flow. |
|`corelight.suricata.tx_id` | `long` | Index of the transaction inside the flow the alert was raised on. |
|`corelight.tunnel.tunnel_type` | `keyword` | Encapsulation in use, e.g. Tunnel::TEREDO, Tunnel::GRE, Tunnel::IP. |
|`corelight.uid` | `keyword` | Zeek connection unique identifier (uid), used to pivot across logs. |
|`corelight.uids` | `array` | Zeek connection identifiers the entry was observed on. |
|`corelight.unknown_protocols.analyzer` | `keyword` | Analyzer that was handling the traffic when the unknown protocol was met. |
|`corelight.unknown_protocols.analyzer_history` | `array` | Chain of analyzers the traffic went through, outermost first. |
|`corelight.unknown_protocols.first_bytes` | `keyword` | First bytes of the unrecognised payload, hex encoded. |
|`corelight.unknown_protocols.protocol_id` | `keyword` | Numeric identifier of the protocol that could not be recognised. |
|`corelight.unknown_protocols.protocol_name` | `keyword` | Name of the protocol that could not be recognised, when one is known. |
|`corelight.weird.addl` | `keyword` | Additional context about the anomaly. |
|`corelight.weird.name` | `keyword` | Identifier of the protocol anomaly Zeek detected, e.g. JA3_repeat_different. |
|`corelight.weird.notice` | `boolean` | Whether the anomaly was also raised as a notice. |
|`corelight.weird.peer` | `keyword` | Zeek worker that reported the anomaly. |
|`corelight.x509.basic_constraints_ca` | `boolean` | Whether the basic constraints extension marks the certificate as a CA. |
|`corelight.x509.basic_constraints_path_length` | `long` | Path length constraint carried by the certificate's basic constraints extension. |
|`corelight.x509.client_cert` | `boolean` | Whether the certificate was presented by the client. |
|`corelight.x509.host_cert` | `boolean` | Whether the certificate belongs to the host it was presented for. |
|`corelight.x509.key_type` | `keyword` | Family of the public key: rsa, dsa or ecdsa. |
|`destination.as.number` | `long` | Unique number allocated to the autonomous system. |
|`destination.as.organization.name` | `keyword` | Organization name. |
|`destination.bytes` | `long` | Bytes sent from the destination to the source. |
|`destination.domain` | `keyword` | The domain name of the destination. |
|`destination.geo.city_name` | `keyword` | City name. |
|`destination.geo.country_iso_code` | `keyword` | Country ISO code. |
|`destination.geo.country_name` | `keyword` | Country name. |
|`destination.geo.region_name` | `keyword` | Region name. |
|`destination.ip` | `ip` | IP address of the destination. |
|`destination.mac` | `keyword` | MAC address of the destination. |
|`destination.packets` | `long` | Packets sent from the destination to the source. |
|`destination.port` | `long` | Port of the destination. |
|`dns.answers` | `object` | Array of DNS answers. |
|`dns.header_flags` | `keyword` | Array of DNS header flags. |
|`dns.id` | `keyword` | The DNS packet identifier assigned by the program that generated the query. The identifier is copied to the response. |
|`dns.question.class` | `keyword` | The class of records being queried. |
|`dns.question.name` | `keyword` | The name being queried. |
|`dns.question.registered_domain` | `keyword` | The highest registered domain, stripped of the subdomain. |
|`dns.question.type` | `keyword` | The type of record being queried. |
|`dns.response_code` | `keyword` | The DNS response code. |
|`email.cc.address` | `keyword` | Email address of CC recipient |
|`email.from.address` | `keyword` | The sender's email address. |
|`email.message_id` | `wildcard` | Value from the Message-ID header. |
|`email.reply_to.address` | `keyword` | Address replies should be delivered to. |
|`email.sender.address` | `keyword` | Address of the message sender. |
|`email.subject` | `keyword` | The subject of the email message. |
|`email.to.address` | `keyword` | Email address of recipient |
|`error.code` | `keyword` | Error code describing the error. |
|`error.message` | `match_only_text` | Error message. |
|`event.action` | `keyword` | The action captured by the event. |
|`event.category` | `keyword` | Event category. The second categorization field in the hierarchy. |
|`event.dataset` | `keyword` | Name of the dataset. |
|`event.duration` | `long` | Duration of the event in nanoseconds. |
|`event.kind` | `keyword` | The kind of the event. The highest categorization field in the hierarchy. |
|`event.module` | `keyword` | Name of the module this data is coming from. |
|`event.outcome` | `keyword` | The outcome of the event. The lowest level categorization field in the hierarchy. |
|`event.severity` | `long` | Numeric severity of the event. |
|`event.type` | `keyword` | Event type. The third categorization field in the hierarchy. |
|`file.accessed` | `date` | Last time the file was accessed. |
|`file.created` | `date` | File creation time. |
|`file.ctime` | `date` | Last time the file attributes or metadata changed. |
|`file.directory` | `keyword` | Directory where the file is located. |
|`file.hash.md5` | `keyword` | MD5 hash. |
|`file.hash.sha1` | `keyword` | SHA1 hash. |
|`file.hash.sha256` | `keyword` | SHA256 hash. |
|`file.mime_type` | `keyword` | Media type of file, document, or arrangement of bytes. |
|`file.mtime` | `date` | Last time the file content was modified. |
|`file.name` | `keyword` | Name of the file including the extension, without the directory. |
|`file.path` | `keyword` | Full path to the file, including the file name. |
|`file.pe.architecture` | `keyword` | CPU architecture target for the file. |
|`file.size` | `long` | File size in bytes. |
|`file.x509.alternative_names` | `keyword` | List of subject alternative names (SAN). |
|`file.x509.issuer.distinguished_name` | `keyword` | Distinguished name (DN) of issuing certificate authority. |
|`file.x509.not_after` | `date` | Time at which the certificate is no longer considered valid. |
|`file.x509.not_before` | `date` | Time at which the certificate is first considered valid. |
|`file.x509.public_key_algorithm` | `keyword` | Algorithm used to generate the public key. |
|`file.x509.public_key_curve` | `keyword` | The curve used by the elliptic curve public key algorithm. This is algorithm specific. |
|`file.x509.public_key_exponent` | `long` | Exponent used to derive the public key. This is algorithm specific. |
|`file.x509.public_key_size` | `long` | The size of the public key space in bits. |
|`file.x509.serial_number` | `keyword` | Unique serial number issued by the certificate authority. |
|`file.x509.signature_algorithm` | `keyword` | Identifier for certificate signature algorithm. |
|`file.x509.subject.distinguished_name` | `keyword` | Distinguished name (DN) of the certificate subject entity. |
|`file.x509.version_number` | `keyword` | Version of x509 format. |
|`host.hostname` | `keyword` | Hostname of the host. |
|`host.ip` | `ip` | Host ip addresses. |
|`host.mac` | `keyword` | Host MAC addresses. |
|`host.name` | `keyword` | Name of the host. |
|`http.request.body.bytes` | `long` | Size in bytes of the request body. |
|`http.request.body.content` | `wildcard` | The full HTTP request body. |
|`http.request.method` | `keyword` | HTTP request method. |
|`http.request.mime_type` | `keyword` | Mime type of the body of the request. |
|`http.request.referrer` | `keyword` | Referrer for this HTTP request. |
|`http.response.body.bytes` | `long` | Size in bytes of the response body. |
|`http.response.mime_type` | `keyword` | Mime type of the body of the response. |
|`http.response.status_code` | `long` | HTTP response status code. |
|`http.version` | `keyword` | HTTP version. |
|`network.community_id` | `keyword` | A hash of source and destination IPs and ports. |
|`network.direction` | `keyword` | Direction of the network traffic. |
|`network.iana_number` | `keyword` | IANA Protocol Number. |
|`network.protocol` | `keyword` | Application protocol name. |
|`network.transport` | `keyword` | Protocol Name corresponding to the field `iana_number`. |
|`observer.hostname` | `keyword` | Hostname of the observer. |
|`observer.name` | `keyword` | Custom name of the observer. |
|`observer.product` | `keyword` | The product name of the observer. |
|`observer.type` | `keyword` | The type of the observer the data is coming from. |
|`observer.vendor` | `keyword` | Vendor name of the observer. |
|`package.name` | `keyword` | Package name |
|`package.version` | `keyword` | Package version |
|`rule.category` | `keyword` | Rule category |
|`rule.id` | `keyword` | Rule ID |
|`rule.name` | `keyword` | Rule name |
|`rule.version` | `keyword` | Rule version |
|`source.as.number` | `long` | Unique number allocated to the autonomous system. |
|`source.as.organization.name` | `keyword` | Organization name. |
|`source.bytes` | `long` | Bytes sent from the source to the destination. |
|`source.domain` | `keyword` | The domain name of the source. |
|`source.geo.city_name` | `keyword` | City name. |
|`source.geo.country_iso_code` | `keyword` | Country ISO code. |
|`source.geo.country_name` | `keyword` | Country name. |
|`source.geo.name` | `keyword` | User-defined description of a location. |
|`source.geo.region_name` | `keyword` | Region name. |
|`source.ip` | `ip` | IP address of the source. |
|`source.mac` | `keyword` | MAC address of the source. |
|`source.packets` | `long` | Packets sent from the source to the destination. |
|`source.port` | `long` | Port of the source. |
|`source.user.domain` | `keyword` | Name of the directory the user is a member of. |
|`source.user.name` | `keyword` | Short name or login of the user. |
|`source.user.roles` | `keyword` | Array of user roles at the time of the event. |
|`threat.indicator.provider` | `keyword` | Indicator provider |
|`threat.indicator.type` | `keyword` | Type of indicator |
|`tls.cipher` | `keyword` | String indicating the cipher used during the current connection. |
|`tls.client.hash.sha256` | `keyword` | Certificate fingerprint using the SHA256 digest of DER-encoded version of certificate offered by the client. |
|`tls.client.issuer` | `keyword` | Distinguished name of subject of the issuer of the x.509 certificate presented by the client. |
|`tls.client.ja3` | `keyword` | A hash that identifies clients based on how they perform an SSL/TLS handshake. |
|`tls.client.server_name` | `keyword` | Hostname the client is trying to connect to. Also called the SNI. |
|`tls.client.subject` | `keyword` | Distinguished name of subject of the x.509 certificate presented by the client. |
|`tls.client.x509.subject.distinguished_name` | `keyword` | Distinguished name (DN) of the certificate subject entity. |
|`tls.curve` | `keyword` | String indicating the curve used for the given cipher, when applicable. |
|`tls.established` | `boolean` | Boolean flag indicating if the TLS negotiation was successful and transitioned to an encrypted tunnel. |
|`tls.next_protocol` | `keyword` | String indicating the protocol being tunneled. |
|`tls.resumed` | `boolean` | Boolean flag indicating if this TLS connection was resumed from an existing TLS negotiation. |
|`tls.server.hash.sha256` | `keyword` | Certificate fingerprint using the SHA256 digest of DER-encoded version of certificate offered by the server. |
|`tls.server.issuer` | `keyword` | Subject of the issuer of the x.509 certificate presented by the server. |
|`tls.server.ja3s` | `keyword` | A hash that identifies servers based on how they perform an SSL/TLS handshake. |
|`tls.server.subject` | `keyword` | Subject of the x.509 certificate presented by the server. |
|`tls.server.x509.alternative_names` | `keyword` | List of subject alternative names (SAN). |
|`tls.server.x509.subject.common_name` | `keyword` | List of common names (CN) of subject. |
|`tls.server.x509.subject.distinguished_name` | `keyword` | Distinguished name (DN) of the certificate subject entity. |
|`tls.version` | `keyword` | Numeric part of the version parsed from the original string. |
|`url.domain` | `keyword` | Domain of the url. |
|`url.original` | `wildcard` | Unmodified original url as seen in the event source. |
|`url.path` | `wildcard` | Path of the request, such as "/search". |
|`url.port` | `long` | Port of the request, such as 443. |
|`user.domain` | `keyword` | Name of the directory the user is a member of. |
|`user.name` | `keyword` | Short name or login of the user. |
|`user_agent.original` | `keyword` | Unparsed user_agent string. |



For more information on the Intake Format, please find the code of the Parser, Smart Descriptions, and Supported Events [here](https://github.com/SEKOIA-IO/intake-formats/tree/main/Corelight/corelight).
